# Bitwrench CSS class inventory

> Generated by `node tools/build-css-class-inventory.js` from the current working tree.  
> This is a **current-state inspection aid**, not a signed-off 2.2 namespace map.

Generated: 2026-08-11T06:36:43.739Z

## Scope

Included: modern core/BCCL structural and themed selectors, classes emitted by all 51 registered component factories, runtime machine-token families, code-editor CSS, util-css class emissions, CLI page CSS, and the legacy `dist/bitwrench.css` channel. Unprefixed selectors are intentionally included because they are collision and migration concerns.

Excluded: repository-local `pages/site.js` chrome and classes owned only by demos/examples/blog pages. Those are application CSS, not classes shipped by the bitwrench library, BCCL, or a package addon.

## How to read this document

- **Owner** is the best current/proposed ownership classification. “BCCL (bare legacy name)” is precisely the namespace debt under review.
- **Proposed build** says where the rule should ship after cleanup. “Core build + full build” means the full build inherits the core rule; “full/BCCL build only” also covers the standalone BCCL style payload.
- **Type** distinguishes reusable utilities/roles from component-specific structure and runtime machine tokens.
- **Components** lists BCCL/core factories whose emitted source or selectors use the class.
- **Purpose / key declarations** summarizes the generated default structural/themed rules; inspect selectors when specificity matters.
- **Usage** counts source/docs/pages/examples/test/tool files, excluding generated `dist/`, `releases/`, and archived material.
